      "solution": "**Identify.** Match List-I with List-II. List-I List-II (Material) (Susceptibility (χ)) A. Diamagnetic I. χ=0 B. Ferromagnetic II. 0 > χ ≥ –1 C. Paramagnetic III. χ >> 1 D. The verified code is **(4) A-II, B-III, C-IV, D-I** — every distractor swaps at least one pair.\n\n**Option scan.**\n✗ (1) A-II, B-I, C-III, D-IV\n✗ (2) A-III, B-II, C-I, D-IV\n✗ (3) A-IV, B-III, C-II, D-I\n✓ (4) A-II, B-III, C-IV, D-I\n\n**Hence proved.** The verified option wording is **(4) A-II, B-III, C-IV, D-I**.\n\n**Eliminate.**\n✗ (1) A-II, B-I, C-III, D-IV — At least one left–right pairing is wrong vs NCERT.\n✗ (2) A-III, B-II, C-I, D-IV — At least one left–right pairing is wrong vs NCERT.\n✗ (3) A-IV, B-III, C-II, D-I — At least one left–right pairing is wrong vs NCERT.",
